Title: Christus Sacrae Scripturae Nucleus: or, Christ the Sum and Substance of all the Holy Scriptures in the Old and New Testament; with a sermon on the resurrection of Our Lord
Creator: August Hermann Francke (1663-1727); translated 'by an Antient Doctor of Physick' (anonymous); preface drawn from Francke's own account of his early life
Publisher: London: printed by J. Downing
Publication Date: 1732
Rights: Public Domain (published 1732, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights field reads "NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT")
Source: Internet Archive item christussacraesc00franiala (https://archive.org/details/christussacraesc00franiala), the item's own christussacraesc00franiala_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: University of California Libraries; host metadata date: 1732. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-16. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: f63e9ea7d4a3c887721d9a01d42752848627694f24eff5aab9685347fcc706f2.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Library noise before the title page is left in place.
Content note: Francke's Christocentric hermeneutic in PD English, and a preface built from papers in which Francke himself described the first part of his life, quoting his 1687 Lüneburg conversion in the first person: the earliest PD English witness to the Lebenslauf.
